## (a)  Purp/uni006Fse /uni006Ff the P/uni006Fst-IP/uni004F Share /uni004Fpti/uni006Fn Scheme

The purp/uni006Fses /uni006Ff the P/uni006Fst-IP/uni004F Share /uni004Fpti/uni006Fn Scheme are t/uni006F attract and retain the best available pers/uni006Fnnel, t/uni006F pr/uni006Fvide additi/uni006Fnal incentive t/uni006F empl/uni006Fyees, direct/uni006Frs, c/uni006Fnsultants and advisers /uni006Ff /uni006Fur Gr/uni006Fup and t/uni006F pr/uni006Fm/uni006Fte the success /uni006Ff the business /uni006Ff /uni006Fur Gr/uni006Fup.

## (b)  Exercise Price and Purchase Price /uni006Ff the P/uni006Fst-IP/uni004F Share /uni004Fpti/uni006Fn Scheme

The am/uni006Funt payable f/uni006Fr  each Share t/uni006F  be subscribed f/uni006Fr under an /uni006Fpti/uni006Fn  in  the  event  /uni006Ff  the  /uni006Fpti/uni006Fn  being exercised shall be determined by the B/uni006Fard and shall be n/uni006Ft less than the greater /uni006Ff

- (i) the  cl/uni006Fsing  price  /uni006Ff  the  Shares  /uni006Fn  the  Main  B/uni006Fard  /uni006Ff  the  St/uni006Fck  Exchange  as  stated  in  the  St/uni006Fck Exchange's daily qu/uni006Ftati/uni006Fns sheet /uni006Fn the date /uni006Ff grant;
- (ii) the average cl/uni006Fsing price /uni006Ff the Shares /uni006Fn the Main B/uni006Fard /uni006Ff the St/uni006Fck Exchange as stated in the St/uni006Fck Exchange's daily qu/uni006Ftati/uni006Fns sheets f/uni006Fr the five business days immediately preceding the date /uni006Ff grant; and
- (iii) the n/uni006Fminal value /uni006Ff the Shares.

## (c)  Lapse /uni006Ff /uni004Fpti/uni006Fn /uni006Fr Right

## 2.1 BASIS /uni004FF PREPARATI/uni004FN

These  financial  statements  have  been  prepared  in  acc/uni006Frdance  with  Internati/uni006Fnal  Financial  Rep/uni006Frting Standards  ('IFRSs'),  which  c/uni006Fmprise all standards  and  interpretati/uni006Fns appr/uni006Fved by  the  Internati/uni006Fnal Acc/uni006Funting  Standards  B/uni006Fard  (the  'IASB')  and  Internati/uni006Fnal  Acc/uni006Funting  Standards  ('IASs')  and  Standing Interpretati/uni006Fns  C/uni006Fmmittee  interpretati/uni006Fns  appr/uni006Fved  by  the  Internati/uni006Fnal  Acc/uni006Funting  Standards  C/uni006Fmmittee that remain in effect, and the discl/uni006Fsure requirements /uni006Ff the H/uni006Fng K/uni006Fng C/uni006Fmpanies /uni004Frdinance. They have been prepared under the hist/uni006Frical c/uni006Fst c/uni006Fnventi/uni006Fn, except f/uni006Fr investment pr/uni006Fperties and financial assets at fair value thr/uni006Fugh pr/uni006Ffit /uni006Fr l/uni006Fss which have been measured at fair value.

## Basis /uni006Ff c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidati/uni006Fn

The c/uni006Fns/uni006Flidated financial statements include the financial statements /uni006Ff the C/uni006Fmpany and its subsidiaries (c/uni006Fllectively  referred  t/uni006F  as  the  'Gr/uni006Fup')  f/uni006Fr  the  year  ended  31  December  2022.  A  subsidiary  is  an  entity (including a structured entity), directly /uni006Fr indirectly, c/uni006Fntr/uni006Flled by the C/uni006Fmpany. C/uni006Fntr/uni006Fl is achieved when the Gr/uni006Fup is exp/uni006Fsed, /uni006Fr has rights, t/uni006F variable returns fr/uni006Fm its inv/uni006Flvement with the investee and has the ability t/uni006F  affect  th/uni006Fse  returns  thr/uni006Fugh  its  p/uni006Fwer  /uni006Fver  the  investee  (i.e.,  existing  rights  that  give  the  Gr/uni006Fup  the current ability t/uni006F direct the relevant activities /uni006Ff the investee).

## Fair value measurement

## Fair value hierarchy (C/uni006Fntinued)

Under the disc/uni006Funted  cash  fl/uni006Fw  meth/uni006Fd,  fair  value  is  estimated  using  assumpti/uni006Fns  regarding  the  benefits and liabilities /uni006Ff /uni006Fwnership /uni006Fver the asset's life including an exit /uni006Fr terminal value. This meth/uni006Fd inv/uni006Flves the pr/uni006Fjecti/uni006Fn  /uni006Ff  a  series  /uni006Ff  cash  fl/uni006Fws  /uni006Fn  a  pr/uni006Fperty  interest. The exit yield is n/uni006Frmally separately determined and differs fr/uni006Fm the disc/uni006Funt rate.

The durati/uni006Fn /uni006Ff the cash fl/uni006Fws and the specific timing /uni006Ff infl/uni006Fws and /uni006Futfl/uni006Fws are determined by events such as  rent  reviews,  lease  renewal  and  related  reletting,  redevel/uni006Fpment  /uni006Fr  refurbishment.  The  appr/uni006Fpriate durati/uni006Fn is driven by market behavi/uni006Fur that is a characteristic /uni006Ff the class /uni006Ff pr/uni006Fperty. The peri/uni006Fdic cash fl/uni006Fw is  estimated as gr/uni006Fss  inc/uni006Fme  less  vacancy,  n/uni006Fn-rec/uni006Fverable  expenses,  c/uni006Fllecti/uni006Fn  l/uni006Fsses,  lease  incentives, maintenance c/uni006Fsts, agent and c/uni006Fmmissi/uni006Fn c/uni006Fsts and /uni006Fther /uni006Fperating and management expenses. The series /uni006Ff  peri/uni006Fdic  net /uni006Fperating inc/uni006Fme, al/uni006Fng with an estimate /uni006Ff the terminal value anticipated at the end /uni006Ff the pr/uni006Fjecti/uni006Fn peri/uni006Fd, is then disc/uni006Funted.

A significant increase/uni002F(decrease) in the estimated rental value and the market rent gr/uni006Fwth rate per annum in is/uni006Flati/uni006Fn  w/uni006Fuld  result  in  a  significant  increase/uni002F(decrease)  in  the  fair  value  /uni006Ff  the  investment  pr/uni006Fperties.
